# Skills · S · part 101

903 entries, si-archive to sigma-feature-run. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **si-archive**: Demote, merge, or archive over-budget convention docs and specifically identified dead or duplicate rules in Codex. Use for log rotation or named cleanup even below budget; exclude new convention repair and initial setup. ([sh5623/self-improvement](https://github.com/sh5623/self-improvement/tree/HEAD/plugins/self-improvement/skills/si-archive) · ★1)
- **si-archive**: Use when a convention document has gone over budget (line count or block count), or when dead and duplicated clauses have piled up. Triggers include "the convention doc is too long", "clean up the conventions", "do an archive pass", the si-improve section 6 check reporting the log cap (15 blocks) exceeded, and an always-loaded doc growing large enough to weigh on session context. Do NOT load for: adding or correcting an individual rule (si-improve owns that), or first-time setup (si-init owns that). ([sh5623/self-improvement](https://github.com/sh5623/self-improvement/tree/HEAD/skills/si-archive) · ★1)
- **si-aspect-explorer**: Use when Brett asks to "try an aspect" or "what aspect for {spirit}". Walks through the spirit's aspects with stat-backed deltas and recommends which to try next based on gaps in his playlog. NOT YET IMPLEMENTED — skeleton only; full logic lands in M4. ([bfowle/spirit-island](https://github.com/bfowle/spirit-island/tree/HEAD/skills/si-aspect-explorer) · ★1)
- **si-assertiveness**: Build assertiveness skills for difficult conversations. ([ichigo137/J-A-R-V-I-S](https://github.com/ichigo137/J-A-R-V-I-S/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/si-assertiveness))
- **si-at-the-table**: Use when Brett is mid-game and describes board state for a tactical question. Format is usually "at the table, {spirit} vs {adversary} L{N}, round {M}, {hand}, {board situation}, question: {question}". Returns options with tradeoffs — never a solve. Advisory, not prescriptive. Anti-alpha by design. ([bfowle/spirit-island](https://github.com/bfowle/spirit-island/tree/HEAD/skills/si-at-the-table) · ★1)
- **si-blame-the-task**: The always-on governor for the pack. When self-assessment is hard — am I right, is this done, do I know this — suspect the information environment, not your capability. Don't introspect harder; ENRICH it (run it, read the real file, check docs, ask) and mine data from the past and data from others. Reach a verdict, but hold it loosely and name what you didn't verify. Applies silently to any claim about your own correctness, completeness, or knowledge; dispatches the sibling si-* skills. ([jpoindexter/self-insight-agent-skills](https://github.com/jpoindexter/self-insight-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/si-blame-the-task))
- **si-calibration-gate**: Confidence is not accuracy. Before asserting a fact or marking work "done," separate how sure you feel from how likely you are right, attach explicit uncertainty, and apply a correction factor. Peak certainty is exactly where error hides. Use before any factual claim, "this is correct/complete/fixed," or confidence estimate. ([jpoindexter/self-insight-agent-skills](https://github.com/jpoindexter/self-insight-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/si-calibration-gate))
- **si-coder**: SI-Coder umbrella skill. Build, improve, and publish web apps from plain-language goals for non-technical users. Route through sc, sc-build, sc-fe, sc-all, sc-provider, or focused UI/UX/DX/AX/provider skills while keeping technical choices and secrets behind a safe abstraction layer. ([rahmanef63/si-coder-agent](https://github.com/rahmanef63/si-coder-agent/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★13)
- **si-combo-drill**: Use when Brett asks for "Spirit Island combo drill" or "practice a spirit pairing". Picks 2 spirits to play as a pairing and suggests an adversary that tests the combo's failure mode. NOT YET IMPLEMENTED — skeleton only; full logic lands in M3. ([bfowle/spirit-island](https://github.com/bfowle/spirit-island/tree/HEAD/skills/si-combo-drill) · ★1)
- **si-company-formation**: Company formation and entity choice in Slovenia for tax year 2026, from the Companies Act (ZGD-1) and the Court Register Act (ZSReg). Covers the main forms (d.o.o., d.d., s.p., partnerships), the EUR 7,500 minimum share capital of a d.o.o. with EUR 50 minimum contributions, at least one quarter of each contribution provided and at least EUR 7,500 provided in aggregate before application, the EUR 25,000 minimum for a d.d., the 50-member cap, registration through a SPOT point or a notary, the ten and five working day court deadlines, no court fees, the annual report deadlines at AJPES (three or… ([openaccountants/openaccountants](https://github.com/openaccountants/openaccountants/tree/HEAD/agent-skills/si-company-formation) · ★498)
- **si-competence-floor**: In domains where you are weak, you are least able to detect that you are weak — the skills needed to do the task are the same skills needed to judge it. In unfamiliar territory, distrust the "looks right" signal and verify externally. Also corrects the expert's mirror error: assuming the user knows what you know. Use on unfamiliar stacks, languages, or domains. ([jpoindexter/self-insight-agent-skills](https://github.com/jpoindexter/self-insight-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/si-competence-floor))
- **si-confidence-build**: Provide evidence-based exercises to build confidence. ([ichigo137/J-A-R-V-I-S](https://github.com/ichigo137/J-A-R-V-I-S/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/si-confidence-build))
- **si-confidence-source**: Audit WHY something feels right before trusting the feeling. Three false cues — fluency (easy to generate), pseudorelevance (knowing a lot around the topic), and inherited self-view ("I'm good at this") — produce confidence unconnected to accuracy. Name the cue; if it's one of these, downgrade and verify. Use whenever a confident answer arrives without an explicit check. ([jpoindexter/self-insight-agent-skills](https://github.com/jpoindexter/self-insight-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/si-confidence-source))
- **si-construe-the-situation**: Most prediction errors are misread situations, not unknown selves. Before claiming "this will work," get the actual situation — read the real file, check the real env/config/versions, look at the real data shape and the error paths — instead of predicting against a clean, abstract, happy-path sketch. Lower confidence in proportion to how much of the real situation you haven't seen. Use before predicting any runtime behavior, claiming code will work, or estimating an outcome. ([jpoindexter/self-insight-agent-skills](https://github.com/jpoindexter/self-insight-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/si-construe-the-situation))
- **si-corporate-income-tax**: Slovenian corporate income tax (davek od dohodkov pravnih oseb, DDPO) for tax year 2026. Covers who pays (resident companies on worldwide profit, non-residents through a Slovenian PE), the 22% rate for 2024 to 2028 under ZORZFS art. 64 (the ZDDPO-2 art. 60 rate is 19%), the taxable base and non-deductible items, the 50% rules for entertainment and provisions, maximum depreciation rates, the R&D (100%), investment (40%) and digital and green (40%) reliefs, losses (five years, 50% of the base) and the 63% overall cap in art. 59.a, the interest limitation rule (30% of EBITDA or EUR 3 million), a… ([openaccountants/openaccountants](https://github.com/openaccountants/openaccountants/tree/HEAD/agent-skills/si-corporate-income-tax) · ★498)
- **si-corrective-feedback**: Feedback is the riskiest thing you do — nearly 40% of feedback interventions make performance WORSE. The difference is altitude: criticism aimed at the task and the next concrete step helps; criticism aimed at the author's worth triggers self-protection and backfires. Attack the code, name the fix, frame the gap as closable. Use when you critique code, review a sub-agent's output, or push back on a user's plan. ([jpoindexter/self-insight-agent-skills](https://github.com/jpoindexter/self-insight-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/si-corrective-feedback))
- **si-cross-border**: Hub skill for crossing the Slovenian border in tax year 2026. Covers tax residence of individuals (registered permanent residence, habitual abode or centre of interests, more than 183 days; ZDoh-2 arts. 6 and 7) and companies (seat or place of effective management; ZDDPO-2 art. 5); permanent establishment (art. 6 and 7); non-residents taxed on Slovenian-source income; 15% withholding on dividends, interest, royalties, Slovenian rent, artists' fees and listed services paid to listed jurisdictions (ZDDPO-2 art. 70); EU directive exemptions (arts. 71 and 72); treaty relief on the KIDO forms; the… ([openaccountants/openaccountants](https://github.com/openaccountants/openaccountants/tree/HEAD/agent-skills/si-cross-border) · ★498)
- **si-daily-challenge**: Use when Brett asks for "today's Spirit Island challenge" or says "/si-daily-challenge". Generates a structured daily drill — spirit × adversary × level × scenario × board × player-count × learning goal — seeded by date, varied per reroll, and informed by his playlog history in ~/repos/spirit-island/data/playlog.csv. ([bfowle/spirit-island](https://github.com/bfowle/spirit-island/tree/HEAD/skills/si-daily-challenge) · ★1)
- **si-datacenter-methodology**: Slovenia (SI) datacenter discovery & audit methodology — how to enumerate, verify, and update Slovenia datacenter projects at municipality (obcina) + administrative unit (upravna enota) granularity, grouped into 12 SURS statistical regions for field work (212 municipalities in the current manifest). Slovenia has no public national data-center registry: enumeration joins PIS/eGraditev construction acts (GD/UD/PG), municipal spatial-planning files (OPN/OPPN), environmental-assessment records (ARSO/MOPE/MNVP), ELES/SODO energy-grid evidence, public procurement (e-JN/enarocanje/TED), AKOS telecom… ([huangzesen/us-dc-intel](https://github.com/huangzesen/us-dc-intel/tree/HEAD/scripts/expansion/world/country-skills/SI) · ★1)
- **si-declutter-mind**: Help organize and clear mental clutter through structured thinking. ([ichigo137/J-A-R-V-I-S](https://github.com/ichigo137/J-A-R-V-I-S/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/si-declutter-mind))
- **si-define-the-criterion**: Vague success words — done, clean, secure, fast, good, robust — get defined self-servingly to flatter whatever you produced. Pin the criterion to concrete, observable, falsifiable terms drawn from the task, BEFORE doing the work, so the bar can't move to meet the output. Use whenever success is stated in an ambiguous trait word. ([jpoindexter/self-insight-agent-skills](https://github.com/jpoindexter/self-insight-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/si-define-the-criterion))
- **si-digital-detox**: Plan a meaningful break from digital devices. ([ichigo137/J-A-R-V-I-S](https://github.com/ichigo137/J-A-R-V-I-S/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/si-digital-detox))
- **si-duan-kuo-chong**: 何时调用：当用户需要将内在的善端（恻隐、羞恶、恭敬、是非）有意识地培养壮大时。 关键信号："怎么培养善心？"、"善端怎么壮大？"、"怎么让道德成长？"。 何时不调用：用户需要的是理解性善论（用 xing-shan-lun），或用户需要的是浩然之气（用 hao-ran-zhi-qi）。 ([jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills](https://github.com/jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills/tree/HEAD/meng-zi/si-duan-kuo-chong) · ★1)
- **si-egocentric-check**: You overweight your own frame and forget the other party — the user, another system, the competing process — operates under the same constraints you do. Before any comparative, "this is better/unique/special," or controllability claim, explicitly model the other side. Use on relative judgments, uniqueness claims, and estimates of how much you control an outcome. ([jpoindexter/self-insight-agent-skills](https://github.com/jpoindexter/self-insight-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/si-egocentric-check))
- **si-el-lo-hace-tu-tambien**: Use before giving your own technical justification when it rests mainly on popularity, trend, convention, or what another codebase does rather than on the requirements at hand. Name the requirement and the trade-off instead. NOT when the user already chose the tool; use porque-yo-lo-digo. ([mcasillas17/mexican-mom](https://github.com/mcasillas17/mexican-mom/tree/HEAD/skills/si-el-lo-hace-tu-tambien) · ★2)
- **si-emotion-regulate**: Teach emotional intelligence and regulation techniques. ([ichigo137/J-A-R-V-I-S](https://github.com/ichigo137/J-A-R-V-I-S/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/si-emotion-regulate))
- **si-fear-overcome**: Develop strategies to overcome specific fears and phobias. ([ichigo137/J-A-R-V-I-S](https://github.com/ichigo137/J-A-R-V-I-S/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/si-fear-overcome))
- **si-feedback-integrity**: A passing signal rarely proves what it seems to. Feedback is probabilistic, incomplete, hidden, ambiguous, absent, or biased — and the default habit is to accept what flatters and explain away what doesn't. Before treating a green test, a "looks good," or a success as proof, ask what it does NOT establish, and seek the disconfirming signal. Use when a result is about to be read as validation. ([jpoindexter/self-insight-agent-skills](https://github.com/jpoindexter/self-insight-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/si-feedback-integrity))
- **si-focus-improve**: Improve concentration and deep work capability. ([ichigo137/J-A-R-V-I-S](https://github.com/ichigo137/J-A-R-V-I-S/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/si-focus-improve))
- **si-goal-setting**: Transform vague goals into specific SMART goals. ([ichigo137/J-A-R-V-I-S](https://github.com/ichigo137/J-A-R-V-I-S/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/si-goal-setting))
- **si-gratitude-practice**: Guide a gratitude journaling and appreciation practice. ([ichigo137/J-A-R-V-I-S](https://github.com/ichigo137/J-A-R-V-I-S/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/si-gratitude-practice))
- **si-habit-stack**: Design habit stacks using the James Clear method. ([ichigo137/J-A-R-V-I-S](https://github.com/ichigo137/J-A-R-V-I-S/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/si-habit-stack))
- **si-improve**: Repair a recurring, evidenced convention or documentation gap found during work in Codex. Use when missing, wrong, or ambiguous guidance caused a concrete problem; exclude one-off code mistakes, initial setup, and archive-only cleanup. ([sh5623/self-improvement](https://github.com/sh5623/self-improvement/tree/HEAD/plugins/self-improvement/skills/si-improve) · ★1)
- **si-improve**: Use when a convention, document, or procedure was missing, wrong, or ambiguous and it cost you time during the work, and that gap looks likely to recur in other files, features, or tasks. Also use when the end-of-work check ("of what bit me, what will recur?") surfaces a gap, and on requests like "self-improvement", "fix the convention", "we have a convention gap". Do NOT load for: one-off mistakes, fixing the code bug itself, trimming bloated convention docs (si-archive owns that), or first-time setup in a project without the system (si-init owns that). ([sh5623/self-improvement](https://github.com/sh5623/self-improvement/tree/HEAD/skills/si-improve) · ★1)
- **si-init**: Bootstrap or register a project's convention repair system in Codex, preserving existing records and separating Codex instruction wiring from Claude. Use for initial setup or checking the setup after a plugin update. ([sh5623/self-improvement](https://github.com/sh5623/self-improvement/tree/HEAD/plugins/self-improvement/skills/si-init) · ★1)
- **si-init**: Use to bootstrap a project once, when it has no self-improvement data file (convention changelog and routing table). Triggers include "set up self-improvement", "si-init", and si-improve / si-archive / convention-smith failing to find the data file. Also re-run it right after a plugin update — it compares the data file's version stamp and repairs stale template wording (project data is never touched). Re-running is safe and idempotent: when it detects an existing system it registers and repairs rather than overwriting. ([sh5623/self-improvement](https://github.com/sh5623/self-improvement/tree/HEAD/skills/si-init) · ★1)

- **si-ju-jiao**: 何时调用：当用户需要一套完整的日常修行框架，或在"修心"过程中需要具体的操作步骤时。 关键信号："怎么修心？""日常怎么练？""心学具体怎么操作？"。 何时不调用：用户需要的是哲学基础（用 xin-ji-li），或用户需要的是知行合一（用 zhi-xing-he-yi）。 ([jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills](https://github.com/jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills/tree/HEAD/yang-ming-xin-xue/si-ju-jiao) · ★1)

- **si-kao-kuai-yu-man**: 当用户出现判断偏差、决策失误、被直觉误导,或想理解"双系统思维/认知偏差/前景理论"时调用。适用于决策审查、风险评估、避免营销/偏见陷阱、个人反省;不适用于需要精确统计/数学推导的纯量化场景(此处提供直觉层矫正)。 ([jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills](https://github.com/jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills/tree/HEAD/si-kao-kuai-yu-man) · ★1)
- **si-kao-kuai-yu-man-zong-gang**: 《思考,快与慢》全书串联总览:把"双系统—三大启发式—前景/框架—过度自信/回归—两个自我—慢思考训练"子技能整合为一套可操作的偏差审查体系。当需要整体把握本书、对具体决策做全维度偏差体检,或对照《薄伽梵歌》行动哲学时调用。 ([jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills](https://github.com/jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills/tree/HEAD/si-kao-kuai-yu-man/si-kao-kuai-yu-man-zong-gang) · ★1)

- **si-outside-view**: For any estimate or prediction — time, effort, success likelihood, will-this-work — the inside view (spinning a scenario of how it goes) is systematically optimistic. Switch to the outside view: reference class, base rates, past data; predict the typical case, not the best case; apply a correction factor. The direct fix for the planning fallacy. Use on every estimate, ETA, or "this should work. ([jpoindexter/self-insight-agent-skills](https://github.com/jpoindexter/self-insight-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/si-outside-view))

- **sicherheits-check**: Prüft einen Linux-Server (VPS) nur lesend auf Sicherheitsprobleme - von außen offene Ports (auch die, die Docker an der Firewall vorbei öffnet), SSH-Einstellungen, veraltete Software in Containern (PHP, Node, n8n, Traefik, LiteLLM), Abstürze in Logs, offen lesbare Secrets - und gleicht die gefundenen Versionen mit aktuellen Sicherheitslücken ab. Nutzen, wenn jemand seinen Server prüfen oder absichern will, nach Meldungen wie PHP2Shell, oder als regelmäßiger Check. Führt je Projekt eine Historie über gefundene und behobene Befunde. Trigger; "sicherheitscheck", "/sicherheits-check", "prüf die sicherheit", "security check", "ist das sicher". ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/sigma-factor-regulon-inference))
- **sigma-feature-run**: Sigma's standard for running a feature end-to-end across tiered Claude models with delegated verification, recon and review-gathering. Use this skill whenever the user starts, plans, executes, verifies or reviews a feature run; whenever they mention orchestration, subagents, agent tiers, model routing, effort levels, context budget, or "which model should do this"; and whenever a task is large enough to span planning, implementation and verification in one session — even if they don't say the word "orchestration".
